# Fleet Activity Log

Maintained by **project-manager**. One brief entry per agent per session; newest on top. Target: keep the three most recent sessions here, older entries roll to `docs/_archive/ACTIVITY_LOG_2026H1.md` — note that `docs/_archive/` is write-frozen by the docs-guard hook, so rolling entries out needs Marty to lift the guard for that move (the 2026-08-19 WO02 entry below is queued to roll first).

## 2026-08-20 — WO11: Acceptance-walk defects + Home/Git/Project/Avatars (4 build lanes + 3 doctrines + tester + tech-lead audit; all 16 defects fixed; 3 crashes remain UNPROVEN and open)

Directed by tech-lead under `/ultracode` dispatcher. **Four lanes + doctrines delivered per frozen contract** (`docs/design/WO11_CONTRACT.md` + amendments §10, §11, §12): R1 (git: init + .gitignore composer with presets, no dependencies), R2 (agents backend: avatars + memory status probe + Rust-side write-safety mutex for .claude/agents/ + .claude/skills/), UI-A (wizards: project types + list-field space/newline fix + A4 disclosure), UI-B (shell + rail: Home button with session warn, hierarchy row shape fix, rail project row, wizard entry points), UI-C (Inspector: section order, project panel, agent-delete seam, path-safety fix), UI-D (agent properties: autosave debounce + FIFO queue fix, agent editor without Save button, avatars UI, fleet-view detail, tools dropdown scroll fix, model catalog dedup, Markdown-tab agent branch). **Stage 0 blocking:** global `ErrorBoundary` (renders stack + message + Reload; no silent failure on B2/C1/F1). **Tech-lead audit identified 3 UNPROVEN crashes** (B2 agent selection, C1 adopt-to-graph, F1 assemble-after-close): all confirmed code defects on those paths FIXED (bare-`/` split → `canonPath`, `sameRelPath` path comparison in delete listeners, CodeMirror rebuild prevention), but crashes themselves remain UNPROVEN from headless shell — ErrorBoundary now renders the stack on any throw, making future live runs definitive. Invoke **66→73** (+7: git_status/git_init/gitignore_write, agent_avatar_set/read/clear, agent_memory_status). Rust tests **553→586 lib + 18 CLI = 604** (51 new). **Tech-lead §10 ruling** (D5 delete button removal): Inspector keeps no agent-file delete; one writer per file via rail context menu only. Fixed orphaned-node bug with new `agentDeleteListeners` seam (`agents.ts` → `graph.ts`, uses `sameRelPath` to match backslash-stored paths). Fixed two stray path-comparison defects in same hunk. **Tech-lead §11 ruling** (TOCTOU serialization): one module-scope `AGENT_FS: Mutex<()>` in `agents.rs`, guarding 16 commands (agents + skills + avatars + memory ensure), **no `.expect()` — recovery via `unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner())`** (deliberate: assemble.rs's `.expect("assemble queue mutex")` chain is the F1 hypothesis). Tests: `agent_rename_during_save_never_resurrects_the_old_file` (25 trials, real OS threads, deterministic pass every time). **Tech-lead §12 ruling** (Markdown-tab second-writer bypass): **one writer per file doctrine**, recorded in TERMINOLOGY.md. `MarkdownTab` rerouted for agent paths (load from store, save via `saveAgentRaw` queue), not `read_md_file`/`write_md_file`. `write_md_file` guards agent paths (Rust rejection arm). `setTab` flushes pending saves and resets on selection change. `MemoryNodeCard`'s "Create file" → `agent_create` for agents (real template). **Defect tally in WO11**: 16 found across all lanes (8 HIGH/MAJOR + 8 MEDIUM/LOW); ALL FIXED. Tester found 4 in audit (1 HIGH, 1 MAJOR, 2 MEDIUM), plus 1 HIGH on re-verification (TOCTOU window); R2 found 1 HIGH (AGENT_FS mutex gap); UI-C found 2 (delete self-conflict + render-batching); UI-D found 4th writer bypass (Markdown tab). **Three HIGH from same seam**: D4 autosave queue without audit of all other writers (Markdown tab, Review revert, Create-file) → three data-loss paths, all now one-writer. **Process incident recorded**: UI-A wrote temporary stub files into another lane's zone (src/git/), broke R1's landed work — stubs deleted before commit but unrecoverable from git. Rule: **never write scratch into another lane's zone**. **Manual written:** `docs/testing/WO11_TEST_MANUAL.md` (1072 lines, sections A–M plus four subsections, 109 steps, including B's one-pass crash capture, G.3/G.6/G.7 race/concurrency tests, J.1 CRLF fix verification, K avatar 2GB-sparse-file stat-first rejection). **Final gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0, `npm run lint` 0 errors (7 pre-existing react-refresh warnings), `npm run build` ✓, `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean, **`cargo test` 604/604 (586 lib + 18 CLI), 0 failed**, **invoke contract 73/73** declared, registered, TS-called exact. Idle by scope: tech-barn, product-analyst. **Awaiting:** Marty's manual acceptance walks (WO11 + WO10 + WO09 + WO03 + WO02 + WO01 all open). Uncommitted.

## 2026-08-19 — WO09: Canvas Connectors, Round 2 (one build lane + tester gates; defects found + fixed in-session, feature-complete)

Directed by tech-lead under `/ultracode` dispatcher. **Round 1 (uncommitted)** received Marty's rejection on 2026-08-19 with two findings: (1) connection lines still too far from Input connectors, (2) connectors should be taller and resemble 16-bit video game cartridge contacts. **Root cause identified by tech-lead:** React Flow's `getHandlePosition` returns handle border-box outer edge (not centre), causing `edgePath.ts` to double-count socket geometry with a stale `SOCKET_GAP = 10` constant. **Frozen contract: `docs/design/WO09_CONNECTOR_CONTRACT.md` (420 lines, §3 constant table as single source of truth: `CONN_H 44`, `FINGER_COUNT 5`, `FINGER_PITCH 8`, `RISER_STEP 8`, `NODE_H 128`; wire plugs in at `targetX + SOCKET_BITE(3)`, arrowhead tip deliberately swallowed by socket with 8/11 px readable; path start at `sourceX + PIN_REACH(10)`; persistent per-edge slots deferred but geometry-aware ordering, lane routing, custom preview all scoped OUT for v0.1.0).** **Tech-ui one lane, 8 files, zero deviations:** NEW `src/canvas/portSlots.ts` (assignPortSlots pure rank on string compare, `SLOT_COUNT 5`, overflow wrap rule); `edgePath.ts` re-signed to one EdgeEnds object with slot-shifted endpoints; `MemoryEdge.tsx` passes slots, `interactionWidth` 16→12; `GraphCanvas.tsx` calls assignPortSlots once in edges effect; `types.ts` adds inSlot/outSlot; `MemoryNodeCard.tsx` comment only; CSS `CONN_H 24→44` with `repeating-linear-gradient` cartridge fingers via `--contact` custom property on `.ct-port-{in,out}`. **Tester: all five gates green** — `npm run build` clean, `npm run lint` 0 errors (1 pre-existing RoleGlyphs warning), `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean, `cargo test` **542 passed** (524 lib + 18 CLI, 0 failed), **invoke contract 63/63 byte-exact** (both sets extracted, `comm`'d, Rust untouched this round confirms no regression). `markerUnits="userSpaceOnUse"` zoom question resolved by reading `@xyflow/react` source: React Flow uses one CSS `transform: scale()` on viewport wrapper, so paths and markers scale as one pixel image, no zoom gap. Tester verified `assignPortSlots` genuinely pure (node moves never touch domainEdges). **2 MINOR defects found + FIXED in-session** (both stale comments, pre-existing blamed to commit 7122ee1 but in files this lane owns for comment refresh): MemoryNodeCard.tsx:13-16 described nonexistent three-handles-per-side + `picks` architecture contradicting real single-handle/no-id JSX; MemoryEdge.tsx:8-14 had per-kind stroke table stale vs real const (2px/4px claimed vs 3px/5px actual, every dash off by one). Fix round changed comment text only; STROKE, JSX, handle wiring byte-identical. `npm run build` + `npm run lint` re-run clean after. **Manual written: `docs/testing/WO09_TEST_MANUAL.md` (45 steps A–J, covering contract §10's eight on-screen checks, four reject conditions §10.1/§10.2/§10.4/§10.7, three zoom levels 50%/100%/200%, state-colour cascade, with sign-off table).** Feature-complete; gates green; awaiting Marty's manual acceptance walk. Idle by scope: tech-general, tech-barn, product-analyst. Uncommitted. **Note: INPUT_PROMPT's earlier brainstorm block (08/19 2:10 PM) lists 11 items including pathfinding, hierarchy panel, wizards, project properties, orchestrator tab; round-1 work and `src/orchestrator/` appear to address some but are out-of-scope for WO09 — logged to backlog.**
